Današnji sistem takmičenja se sastoji od šesnaest ekipa koja igra svaka sa svakim dva puta u regularnom delu nakon kojeg sledi plejof osam najboljih ekipa. Iz prve lige ispadaju dve najslabije plasirane ekipe. |

FIBA had the main say in this competition until 2000 when the clubs took control of the competition through their association Eurolegaue Basketball. The format of the competition has changed throughout history and included a group stage, followed by eliminations in the playoffs. The winner of this competition is decided at the final tournament called the Final Four. The most successful clubs in this competition are Real Madrid, CSKA Moscow, Maccabi Tel-Aviv, Panathinaikos, Varese, Olympiakos. |
